namespace = ssp_standalone

fleet_event = {
 id = ssp_standalone.1
 hide_window = yes
 is_triggered_only = yes
 trigger = { exists = controller controller = { foc_v1717c_imperial_strategic_aircraft_allowed = yes } }
 immediate = {
  log = "SSP_STANDALONE: imperial action completed"
  save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_source_fleet
  controller = { save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_owner }
  solar_system = { save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_source_system }
  event_target:ssp_imperial_owner = {
   random_system = { limit = { distance = { source = event_target:ssp_imperial_source_system min_jumps = 1 max_jumps = 2 } NOT = { has_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected } } save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_target_1 set_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected }
   random_system = { limit = { distance = { source = event_target:ssp_imperial_source_system min_jumps = 1 max_jumps = 2 } NOT = { has_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected } } save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_target_2 set_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected }
   random_system = { limit = { distance = { source = event_target:ssp_imperial_source_system min_jumps = 1 max_jumps = 2 } NOT = { has_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected } } save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_target_3 set_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected }
   random_system = { limit = { distance = { source = event_target:ssp_imperial_source_system min_jumps = 1 max_jumps = 2 } NOT = { has_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected } } save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_target_4 set_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected }
   random_system = { limit = { distance = { source = event_target:ssp_imperial_source_system min_jumps = 1 max_jumps = 2 } NOT = { has_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected } } save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_target_5 set_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected }
   random_system = { limit = { distance = { source = event_target:ssp_imperial_source_system min_jumps = 1 max_jumps = 2 } NOT = { has_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected } } save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_target_6 set_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected }
   random_system = { limit = { distance = { source = event_target:ssp_imperial_source_system min_jumps = 1 max_jumps = 2 } NOT = { has_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected } } save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_target_7 set_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected }
   random_system = { limit = { distance = { source = event_target:ssp_imperial_source_system min_jumps = 1 max_jumps = 2 } NOT = { has_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected } } save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_target_8 set_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected }
   random_system = { limit = { distance = { source = event_target:ssp_imperial_source_system min_jumps = 1 max_jumps = 2 } NOT = { has_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected } } save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_target_9 set_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected }
   random_system = { limit = { distance = { source = event_target:ssp_imperial_source_system min_jumps = 1 max_jumps = 2 } NOT = { has_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected } } save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_target_10 set_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected }
   random_system = { limit = { distance = { source = event_target:ssp_imperial_source_system min_jumps = 1 max_jumps = 2 } NOT = { has_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected } } save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_target_11 set_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected }
   random_system = { limit = { distance = { source = event_target:ssp_imperial_source_system min_jumps = 1 max_jumps = 2 } NOT = { has_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected } } save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_target_12 set_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected }
   every_system = { limit = { has_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected } remove_star_flag = ssp_imperial_selected }
   country_event = { id = ssp_standalone.2 }
  }
 }
}

system_event = {
 id = ssp_standalone.3
 hide_window = yes
 is_triggered_only = yes
 immediate = {
  log = "SSP_STANDALONE: spawning imperial strike"
  random_system_planet = { limit = { is_star = yes } save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_target_star }
  if = { limit = { exists = event_target:ssp_imperial_source_fleet exists = event_target:ssp_imperial_owner exists = event_target:ssp_imperial_target_star }
   event_target:ssp_imperial_owner = {
    create_fleet = {
     name = "SSP_IMPERIAL_TIE_STRIKE_FLEET"
     settings = { uses_naval_capacity = no can_upgrade = no can_disband = yes can_change_composition = yes can_change_leader = no spawn_debris = no }
     effect = {
      set_owner = event_target:ssp_imperial_owner
      set_location = event_target:ssp_imperial_target_star
      save_event_target_as = ssp_imperial_imperial_tie_fleet
      event_target:ssp_imperial_source_fleet = { every_owned_ship = {
 foc_v236_tie_lowest_dispatch = yes

       if = { limit = { has_component = SSP_IMPERIAL_TIE_HANGAR_01 } event_target:ssp_imperial_imperial_tie_fleet = { create_ship = { name = "SSP_STRATEGIC_STRIKE_SQUADRON_1" design = ssp_imperial_tie_platform upgradable = no effect = { set_ship_flag = ssp_remote_carrier_standalone } } } }

fleet_event = { id = ssp_standalone.80 hide_window = yes is_triggered_only = yes immediate = { log = "SSP_STANDALONE: 180-day cleanup" destroy_fleet = this } }
country_event = {
 id = ssp_standalone.90 hide_window = yes is_triggered_only = yes
 immediate = {
  every_owned_fleet = { limit = { has_fleet_flag = ssp_strike_fleet NOT = { has_fleet_flag = ssp_strike_active } } destroy_fleet = this }
 }
}

# Initial selection and unified settings menus.
# Compatibility redirect: old versions called event 100 directly.
country_event = {
 id = ssp_standalone.100
 hide_window = yes
 is_triggered_only = yes
 immediate = { country_event = { id = ssp_standalone.110 } }
}

country_event = {
 id = ssp_standalone.101
 hide_window = yes
 is_triggered_only = yes
 trigger = { is_ai = yes }
 immediate = {
  foc_sync_ssp_strength_to_main_difficulty = yes
  if = { limit = { NOR = { has_country_flag = ssp_size_original has_country_flag = ssp_size_2x has_country_flag = ssp_size_5x has_country_flag = ssp_size_10x } } set_country_flag = ssp_size_original }
  if = {
   limit = { foc_v137_is_story_starfighter_country = yes }
   foc_v137_sync_story_starfighter_access = yes
  }
  else_if = {
   limit = { NOR = { has_country_flag = ssp_fighter_group_all has_country_flag = ssp_fighter_group_empire has_country_flag = ssp_fighter_group_rebel has_country_flag = ssp_fighter_group_republic
 has_country_flag = ssp_fighter_group_cis } }
   set_country_flag = ssp_fighter_group_all
  }
 }
}

country_event = {
 id = ssp_standalone.102
 hide_window = yes
 is_triggered_only = yes
 immediate = {
  if = {
   limit = { is_ai = yes }
   country_event = { id = ssp_standalone.101 }
  }
  else = {
   foc_sync_ssp_strength_to_main_difficulty = yes
   if = { limit = { NOR = { has_country_flag = ssp_size_original has_country_flag = ssp_size_2x has_country_flag = ssp_size_5x has_country_flag = ssp_size_10x } } set_country_flag = ssp_size_original }
   if = {
   limit = { foc_v137_is_story_starfighter_country = yes }
   foc_v137_sync_story_starfighter_access = yes
  }
  else_if = {
   limit = { NOR = { has_country_flag = ssp_fighter_group_all has_country_flag = ssp_fighter_group_empire has_country_flag = ssp_fighter_group_rebel has_country_flag = ssp_fighter_group_republic
 has_country_flag = ssp_fighter_group_cis } }
   set_country_flag = ssp_fighter_group_all
  }
   # v62: no second start-up popup; fighter settings live under Star Wars MOD Settings.
   set_country_flag = ssp_initial_settings_opened
  }
 }
}

 }
